# Gap matrix — ARC supply vs TCKDB demand

Generated by `tools/join_inventories.py`. Do not hand-edit; edit the Phase A
inventories and regenerate. Verdict definitions are in `FIELD_KEY.md`.

## Verdict summary

| Verdict | Rows | Meaning |
|---|---:|---|
| `BROKEN` | 8 | adapter maps it but the schema moved under it |
| `ADAPTER_GAP` | 87 | ARC exports it, TCKDB wants it, adapter drops it |
| `ARC_LATENT` | 34 | ARC computes it internally but never exports it |
| `SOURCE_UNCONFIRMED` | 397 | adapter maps it, but not from the export contract (reparse? fabricated?) |
| `ARC_ABSENT` | 1169 | ARC cannot currently produce it |
| `WIRED` | 390 | ARC exports it, adapter maps it, survives the 0.22.0 drift |
| `SURPLUS` | 115 | ARC offers it, TCKDB has no home for it |
| `ORPHAN_MAPPING` | 202 | adapter targets a field absent from TCKDB 0.22.0 |

**Total rows:** 2402

## ⚠ Near-miss paths — repair before trusting the matrix

These paths nearly matched a TCKDB demand path but did not join. Each is
most likely a spelling slip, and each is currently being reported as a gap
that may not exist.

| Agent | Path as written | Nearest demand path | Similarity |
|---|---|---|---:|
| A3 | `reaction_upload.transition_state.calculations[].freq_result.reaction_coordinate_mode_index` | `reaction_upload.transition_state.calculations[].freq_reaction_coordinate_mode_index` | 0.96 |
